== example1
src title Go-Back-N: window 4, frame 2 lost\nlanes Sender Receiver\nseq SW 0..7 window=4 at=Sender\nvar expected = 0\n\nstep Send frames 0-3 without waiting\n  send Sender -> Receiver "0"\n  send Sender -> Receiver "1"\n  send Sender -> Receiver "2" lost\n  timer Sender "T2" start\n  send Sender -> Receiver "3"\nstep Receiver accepts 0 and 1; 3 is out of order and discarded\n  deliver Receiver "0"\n  deliver Receiver "1"\n  set expected = 2\n  discard Receiver "3"\n  send Receiver -> Sender "ACK 0"\n  send Receiver -> Sender "ACK 1"\nstep ACK 1 arrives: window slides to 2-5, send 4 and 5\n  slide SW to=2\n  send Sender -> Receiver "4"\n  send Sender -> Receiver "5"\nstep Timer for frame 2 expires: go back and resend 2-5\n  timer Sender "T2" expire\n  mark SW[2..5] active\n  send Sender -> Receiver "2"\n  send Sender -> Receiver "3"\n  send Sender -> Receiver "4"\n  send Sender -> Receiver "5"

== example2
src title Bubble sort, first pass\narray A = [5, 1, 4, 2]\nvar i = 0\nstep Compare A[0] and A[1]: 5 > 1, swap\n  mark A[0..1] active\n  swap A[0] A[1]\nstep Compare A[1] and A[2]: 5 > 4, swap\n  unmark A[0]\n  set i = 1\n  swap A[1] A[2]\nstep Compare A[2] and A[3]: 5 > 2, swap\n  unmark A[1]\n  set i = 2\n  swap A[2] A[3]\nstep 5 is in its final place\n  unmark A[2]\n  mark A[3] done\n  say "After one pass the largest value has bubbled to the end."
exp title Bubble sort, first pass\narray A = [5, 1, 4, 2]\nvar i = 0\nstep Compare A[0] and A[1]: 5 > 1, swap\n  mark A[0..1] active\n  swap A[0] A[1]\nstep Compare A[1] and A[2]: 5 > 4, swap\n  unmark A[0]\n  set i = 1\n  swap A[1] A[2]\nstep Compare A[2] and A[3]: 5 > 2, swap\n  unmark A[1]\n  set i = 2\n  swap A[2] A[3]\nstep 5 is in its final place\n  unmark A[2]\n  mark A[3] done\n  say "After one pass the largest value has bubbled to the end."

== example4
src title Stop-and-wait vs selective repeat\nscene Stop-and-wait\n  lanes A B\n  step Send frame 0 and wait\n    send A -> B "0" lost\n    timer A "T0" start\n  step Timeout: resend 0\n    timer A "T0" expire\n    send A -> B "0"\n    deliver B "0"\nscene Selective repeat\n  lanes A B\n  seq W 0..5 window=3 at=A\n  step Send 0, 1, 2\n    send A -> B "0" lost\n    send A -> B "1"\n    send A -> B "2"\n    timer A "T0" start\n  step B buffers 1 and 2; only 0 is resent after its timer expires\n    buffer B "1"\n    buffer B "2"\n    timer A "T0" expire\n    send A -> B "0"\n    deliver B "0 1 2"\n    slide W to=3
exp title Stop-and-wait vs selective repeat\nscene Stop-and-wait\n  lanes A B\n  step Send frame 0 and wait\n    send A -> B "0" lost\n    timer A "T0" start\n  step Timeout: resend 0\n    timer A "T0" expire\n    send A -> B "0"\n    deliver B "0"\nscene Selective repeat\n  lanes A B\n  seq W 0..5 window=3 at=A\n  step Send 0, 1, 2\n    send A -> B "0" lost\n    send A -> B "1"\n    send A -> B "2"\n    timer A "T0" start\n  step B buffers 1 and 2; only 0 is resent after its timer expires\n    buffer B "1"\n    buffer B "2"\n    timer A "T0" expire\n    send A -> B "0"\n    deliver B "0 1 2"\n    slide W to=3

src title Reverse a linked list\nlist L = 1 -> 2 -> 3\npointer prev\npointer cur -> L[0]\nstep 1 now points back to prev (null); move both pointers forward\n  link L[0] -> null\n  move prev -> L[0]\n  move cur -> L[1]\nstep 2 points back to 1\n  link L[1] -> L[0]\n  move prev -> L[1]\n  move cur -> L[2]\nstep 3 points back to 2; prev is the new head\n  link L[2] -> L[1]\n  move prev -> L[2]\n  move cur -> null\n  mark L[2] done

src title Breadth-first search from A\ngraph G = A-B, A-C, B-D, C-D, D-E\nqueue Q = [A]\nstep Visit A, put its neighbors B and C in the queue\n  dequeue Q\n  mark G[A] done\n  set G[A] = "d=0"\n  mark G[A-B] active\n  mark G[A-C] active\n  enqueue Q B\n  enqueue Q C\nstep Visit B: D is new\n  dequeue Q\n  mark G[B] done\n  set G[B] = "d=1"\n  mark G[B-D] active\n  enqueue Q D\nstep Visit C: D is already queued\n  dequeue Q\n  mark G[C] done\n  set G[C] = "d=1"\n  mark G[C-D] muted

== gbn-zh
src kit gbn frames=6 window=4 lose=2 lang=zh
exp title 回退 N 步（窗口 4）\nlanes 发送方 接收方\nseq SW 0..5 window=4 at=发送方\nvar 期望 = 0\nstep 发出 0~3\n  send 发送方 -> 接收方 "0"\n  timer 发送方 "T0" start\n  send 发送方 -> 接收方 "1"\n  send 发送方 -> 接收方 "2" lost\n  send 发送方 -> 接收方 "3"\nstep 接收方：交付 0、1；3 失序，丢弃；回 ACK 0、ACK 1、ACK 1（重复确认）\n  deliver 接收方 "0"\n  deliver 接收方 "1"\n  discard 接收方 "3"\n  set 期望 = 2\n  send 接收方 -> 发送方 "ACK 0"\n  send 接收方 -> 发送方 "ACK 1"\n  send 接收方 -> 发送方 "ACK 1"\nstep 收到 ACK 0、ACK 1、ACK 1；窗口滑到 2~5；发出 4、5\n  mark SW[0..1] done\n  timer 发送方 "T0" stop\n  timer 发送方 "T2" start\n  slide SW to=2\n  send 发送方 -> 接收方 "4"\n  send 发送方 -> 接收方 "5"\nstep 接收方：4、5 失序，丢弃；回 ACK 1、ACK 1（重复确认）\n  discard 接收方 "4"\n  discard 接收方 "5"\n  send 接收方 -> 发送方 "ACK 1"\n  send 接收方 -> 发送方 "ACK 1"\nstep 收到 ACK 1、ACK 1；帧 2 超时：从 2 起全部重发\n  timer 发送方 "T2" expire\n  mark SW[2..5] active\n  send 发送方 -> 接收方 "2"\n  timer 发送方 "T2" start\n  send 发送方 -> 接收方 "3"\n  send 发送方 -> 接收方 "4"\n  send 发送方 -> 接收方 "5"\nstep 接收方：交付 2~5；回 ACK 2、ACK 3、ACK 4、ACK 5\n  deliver 接收方 "2"\n  deliver 接收方 "3"\n  deliver 接收方 "4"\n  deliver 接收方 "5"\n  set 期望 = 6\n  send 接收方 -> 发送方 "ACK 2"\n  send 接收方 -> 发送方 "ACK 3"\n  send 接收方 -> 发送方 "ACK 4"\n  send 接收方 -> 发送方 "ACK 5"\nstep 收到 ACK 2、ACK 3、ACK 4、ACK 5；全部帧已确认\n  mark SW[2..5] done\n  timer 发送方 "T2" stop
title 回退 N 步（窗口 4）
steps 8 scenes 1

exp title 停等协议\nlanes 发送方 接收方\nseq SW 0..2 window=1 at=发送方\nvar 期望 = 0\nstep 发出 0\n  send 发送方 -> 接收方 "0"\n  timer 发送方 "T0" start\nstep 接收方：交付 0；回 ACK 0\n  deliver 接收方 "0"\n  set 期望 = 1\n  send 接收方 -> 发送方 "ACK 0"\nstep 收到 ACK 0；窗口滑到 1；发出 1\n  mark SW[0..0] done\n  timer 发送方 "T0" stop\n  slide SW to=1\n  send 发送方 -> 接收方 "1" lost\n  timer 发送方 "T1" start\nstep 帧 1 超时，只重发它\n  timer 发送方 "T1" expire\n  mark SW[1] active\n  send 发送方 -> 接收方 "1"\n  timer 发送方 "T1" start\nstep 接收方：交付 1；回 ACK 1\n  deliver 接收方 "1"\n  set 期望 = 2\n  send 接收方 -> 发送方 "ACK 1"\nstep 收到 ACK 1；窗口滑到 2；发出 2\n  mark SW[1..1] done\n  timer 发送方 "T1" stop\n  slide SW to=2\n  send 发送方 -> 接收方 "2"\n  timer 发送方 "T2" start\nstep 接收方：交付 2；回 ACK 2\n  deliver 接收方 "2"\n  set 期望 = 3\n  send 接收方 -> 发送方 "ACK 2" lost\nstep 帧 2 超时，只重发它\n  timer 发送方 "T2" expire\n  mark SW[2] active\n  send 发送方 -> 接收方 "2"\n  timer 发送方 "T2" start\nstep 接收方：2 是重复帧；回 ACK 2\n  discard 接收方 "2"\n  send 接收方 -> 发送方 "ACK 2"\nstep 收到 ACK 2；全部帧已确认\n  mark SW[2..2] done\n  timer 发送方 "T2" stop

exp title 冒泡排序\narray A = [5, 1, 4, 2, 3]\npointer j -> A[0]\nstep 第 1 趟：5 > 1，交换\n  mark A[0..1] active\n  move j -> A[0]\n  swap A[0] A[1]\nstep 第 1 趟：5 > 4，交换\n  unmark A[0]\n  mark A[1..2] active\n  move j -> A[1]\n  swap A[1] A[2]\nstep 第 1 趟：5 > 2，交换\n  unmark A[1]\n  mark A[2..3] active\n  move j -> A[2]\n  swap A[2] A[3]\nstep 第 1 趟：5 > 3，交换；5 到达最终位置\n  unmark A[2]\n  mark A[3..4] active\n  move j -> A[3]\n  swap A[3] A[4]\n  unmark A[3]\n  mark A[4] done\nstep 第 2 趟：1 ≤ 4，不动\n  mark A[0..1] active\n  move j -> A[0]\nstep 第 2 趟：4 > 2，交换\n  unmark A[0]\n  mark A[1..2] active\n  move j -> A[1]\n  swap A[1] A[2]\nstep 第 2 趟：4 > 3，交换；4 到达最终位置\n  unmark A[1]\n  mark A[2..3] active\n  move j -> A[2]\n  swap A[2] A[3]\n  unmark A[2]\n  mark A[3] done\nstep 第 3 趟：1 ≤ 2，不动\n  mark A[0..1] active\n  move j -> A[0]\nstep 第 3 趟：2 ≤ 3，不动；3 到达最终位置\n  unmark A[0]\n  mark A[1..2] active\n  move j -> A[1]\n  unmark A[1]\n  mark A[2] done\nstep 这一趟没有交换，已经有序\n  mark A done\n  move j -> null

== insertion-zh
src kit insertion A=[b, d, a, c] lang=zh
exp title 直接插入排序\narray A = [b, d, a, c]\npointer key\nstep 取出 A[1] = d，往前找位置\n  move key -> A[1]\n  mark A[1] active\nstep b ≤ d，d 插在这里；前 2 个有序\n  unmark A[1]\nstep 取出 A[2] = a，往前找位置\n  move key -> A[2]\n  mark A[2] active\nstep d > a，d 后移\n  swap A[1] A[2]\n  move key -> A[1]\nstep b > a，b 后移\n  swap A[0] A[1]\n  move key -> A[0]\nstep 到头了，a 放在最前；前 3 个有序\n  unmark A[0]\nstep 取出 A[3] = c，往前找位置\n  move key -> A[3]\n  mark A[3] active\nstep d > c，d 后移\n  swap A[2] A[3]\n  move key -> A[2]\nstep b ≤ c，c 插在这里；前 4 个有序\n  unmark A[2]\nstep 排序完成\n  mark A done\n  move key -> null

exp title 快速排序（枢轴取末尾）\narray A = [5, 1, 4, 2, 8, 3]\npointer i\npointer j\nstep 划分 A[0..5]，枢轴 = 3\n  mark A[5] active\n  move i -> null\n  move j -> A[0]\nstep 5 ≥ 3：跳过\n  move j -> A[0]\nstep 1 < 3：i 前进，交换 A[0] 和 A[1]\n  move j -> A[1]\n  move i -> A[0]\n  swap A[0] A[1]\nstep 4 ≥ 3：跳过\n  move j -> A[2]\nstep 2 < 3：i 前进，交换 A[1] 和 A[3]\n  move j -> A[3]\n  move i -> A[1]\n  swap A[1] A[3]\nstep 8 ≥ 3：跳过\n  move j -> A[4]\nstep 枢轴 3 放到 A[2]，左边都比它小、右边都不小\n  move j -> null\n  move i -> null\n  swap A[2] A[5]\n  mark A[2] done\nstep 划分 A[0..1]，枢轴 = 2\n  mark A[1] active\n  move i -> null\n  move j -> A[0]\nstep 1 < 2：i 前进\n  move j -> A[0]\n  move i -> A[0]\nstep 枢轴 2 放到 A[1]，左边都比它小、右边都不小\n  move j -> null\n  move i -> null\n  mark A[1] done\nstep A[0] 只有一个元素，已就位\n  mark A[0] done\nstep 划分 A[3..5]，枢轴 = 4\n  mark A[5] active\n  move i -> null\n  move j -> A[3]\nstep 5 ≥ 4：跳过\n  move j -> A[3]\nstep 8 ≥ 4：跳过\n  move j -> A[4]\nstep 枢轴 4 放到 A[3]，左边都比它小、右边都不小\n  move j -> null\n  move i -> null\n  swap A[3] A[5]\n  mark A[3] done\nstep 划分 A[4..5]，枢轴 = 5\n  mark A[5] active\n  move i -> null\n  move j -> A[4]\nstep 8 ≥ 5：跳过\n  move j -> A[4]\nstep 枢轴 5 放到 A[4]，左边都比它小、右边都不小\n  move j -> null\n  move i -> null\n  swap A[4] A[5]\n  mark A[4] done\nstep A[5] 只有一个元素，已就位\n  mark A[5] done\nstep 排序完成\n  mark A done

== crc-zh
src kit crc data=101001 gen=1101 lang=zh
exp title CRC 循环冗余检验：101001 / 1101\nsheet 竖式\nwrite 竖式[1,0] "1101"\nwrite 竖式[1,4] ")"\nwrite 竖式[1,5] "101001000"\nrule 竖式[1,5..13] above\nmark 竖式[1,11..13] new\nlabel 竖式[0] "← 商 Q"\nlabel 竖式[1] "← 被除数：M 后补 3 个 0"\ntext "除数 1101 写在左边，从被除数最高位起，每次对齐除数做一次模 2 减（异或）"\nstep G = 1101 对应多项式 x³ + x² + 1，最高次幂是 3，所以 r = 3：校验码 R 有 3 位（= G 的位数 4 − 1）\n  mark 竖式[1,0..3] active\n  say "从左到右每一位对应 x 的 3 次、2 次……0 次，是 1 的位就有这一项"\nstep 在 M = 101001 后补 r = 3 个 0（相当于 M × 2³），得到被除数 101001000\n  unmark 竖式[1,0..3]\n  mark 竖式[1,11..13] active\n  say "补出来的 3 位留给余数：最后把余数填进这 3 位，整个帧就能被 G 整除"\nstep 第 1 次：取 4 位 1010，首位是 1，够除：商上 1，减（异或）1101，得 0111；落下被除数的下一位 0，凑够 4 位\n  unmark 竖式[1,11..13]\n  mark 竖式[1,5..8] active\n  write 竖式[0,8] "1"\n  write 竖式[2,5] "1101"\n  rule 竖式[2,5..8]\n  write 竖式[3,6] "111"\n  say "逐位异或（相同得 0、不同得 1）：1⊕1=0  0⊕1=1  1⊕0=1  0⊕1=1。首位一定变成 0，所以去掉它，只留后 3 位"\n  arrow 竖式[1..3,9]\n  write 竖式[3,9] "0"\nstep 第 2 次：取 4 位 1110，首位是 1，够除：商上 1，减（异或）1101，得 0011；落下被除数的下一位 1，凑够 4 位\n  unmark 竖式[1,5..8]\n  mark 竖式[3,6..9] active\n  write 竖式[0,9] "1"\n  write 竖式[4,6] "1101"\n  rule 竖式[4,6..9]\n  write 竖式[5,7] "011"\n  say "逐位异或（相同得 0、不同得 1）：1⊕1=0  1⊕1=0  1⊕0=1  0⊕1=1。首位一定变成 0，所以去掉它，只留后 3 位"\n  arrow 竖式[1..5,10]\n  write 竖式[5,10] "1"\nstep 第 3 次：取 4 位 0111，首位是 0，不够除：商上 0，减（异或）0000，不变；落下被除数的下一位 0，凑够 4 位\n  unmark 竖式[3,6..9]\n  mark 竖式[5,7..10] active\n  write 竖式[0,10] "0"\n  write 竖式[6,7] "0000"\n  rule 竖式[6,7..10]\n  write 竖式[7,8] "111"\n  say "逐位异或（相同得 0、不同得 1）：0⊕0=0  1⊕0=1  1⊕0=1  1⊕0=1。首位一定变成 0，所以去掉它，只留后 3 位"\n  arrow 竖式[1..7,11]\n  write 竖式[7,11] "0"\nstep 第 4 次：取 4 位 1110，首位是 1，够除：商上 1，减（异或）1101，得 0011；落下被除数的下一位 0，凑够 4 位\n  unmark 竖式[5,7..10]\n  mark 竖式[7,8..11] active\n  write 竖式[0,11] "1"\n  write 竖式[8,8] "1101"\n  rule 竖式[8,8..11]\n  write 竖式[9,9] "011"\n  say "逐位异或（相同得 0、不同得 1）：1⊕1=0  1⊕1=0  1⊕0=1  0⊕1=1。首位一定变成 0，所以去掉它，只留后 3 位"\n  arrow 竖式[1..9,12]\n  write 竖式[9,12] "0"\nstep 第 5 次：取 4 位 0110，首位是 0，不够除：商上 0，减（异或）0000，不变；落下被除数的下一位 0，凑够 4 位\n  unmark 竖式[7,8..11]\n  mark 竖式[9,9..12] active\n  write 竖式[0,12] "0"\n  write 竖式[10,9] "0000"\n  rule 竖式[10,9..12]\n  write 竖式[11,10] "110"\n  say "逐位异或（相同得 0、不同得 1）：0⊕0=0  1⊕0=1  1⊕0=1  0⊕0=0。首位一定变成 0，所以去掉它，只留后 3 位"\n  arrow 竖式[1..11,13]\n  write 竖式[11,13] "0"\nstep 第 6 次：取 4 位 1100，首位是 1，够除：商上 1，减（异或）1101，得 0001\n  unmark 竖式[9,9..12]\n  mark 竖式[11,10..13] active\n  write 竖式[0,13] "1"\n  write 竖式[12,10] "1101"\n  rule 竖式[12,10..13]\n  write 竖式[13,11] "001"\n  say "逐位异或（相同得 0、不同得 1）：1⊕1=0  1⊕1=0  0⊕0=0  0⊕1=1。首位一定变成 0，所以去掉它，只留后 3 位"\nstep 被除数的每一位都用完了，最后剩下的 3 位就是余数 R = 001（不足 3 位也要补齐）；把它接在 M 后面，发送 101001001\n  unmark 竖式[11,10..13]\n  mark 竖式[13,11..13] done\n  label 竖式[13] "← 余数 R，作为 FCS"\n  say "接收方用收到的整个帧除以 1101，余数为 0 就认为没有出错"
title CRC 循环冗余检验：101001 / 1101

src kit bfs G="A-B, A-C, B-D, C-D, D-E" lang=zh
exp title 广度优先搜索（从 A 出发）\ngraph G = A-B, A-C, B-D, C-D, D-E\nqueue 访问序列 = []\nqueue Q = []\nstep 从 A 出发：A 入队\n  mark G[A] active\n  enqueue Q A\nstep A 出队并访问；邻居 B、C 入队\n  dequeue Q\n  mark G[A] done\n  enqueue 访问序列 A\n  mark G[A-B] active\n  mark G[B] active\n  enqueue Q B\n  mark G[A-C] active\n  mark G[C] active\n  enqueue Q C\nstep B 出队并访问；邻居 D 入队\n  dequeue Q\n  mark G[B] done\n  enqueue 访问序列 B\n  mark G[B-D] active\n  mark G[D] active\n  enqueue Q D\nstep C 出队并访问；没有新的邻居\n  dequeue Q\n  mark G[C] done\n  enqueue 访问序列 C\nstep D 出队并访问；邻居 E 入队\n  dequeue Q\n  mark G[D] done\n  enqueue 访问序列 D\n  mark G[D-E] active\n  mark G[E] active\n  enqueue Q E\nstep E 出队并访问；没有新的邻居\n  dequeue Q\n  mark G[E] done\n  enqueue 访问序列 E\nstep 队列空了，遍历结束：A B C D E

== dfs-zh
src kit dfs G="A->B, A->C, B->D, C->D, D->E" from=A lang=zh
exp title 深度优先搜索（从 A 出发）\ngraph G = A->B, A->C, B->D, C->D, D->E\nqueue 访问序列 = []\nstack S = []\nstep 访问 A\n  push S A\n  mark G[A] active\n  enqueue 访问序列 A\nstep 访问 B\n  mark G[A->B] active\n  push S B\n  mark G[B] active\n  enqueue 访问序列 B\nstep 访问 D\n  mark G[B->D] active\n  push S D\n  mark G[D] active\n  enqueue 访问序列 D\nstep 访问 E\n  mark G[D->E] active\n  push S E\n  mark G[E] active\n  enqueue 访问序列 E\nstep E 的邻居都访问过了，回退\n  pop S\n  mark G[E] done\nstep D 的邻居都访问过了，回退\n  pop S\n  mark G[D] done\nstep B 的邻居都访问过了，回退\n  pop S\n  mark G[B] done\nstep 访问 C\n  mark G[A->C] active\n  push S C\n  mark G[C] active\n  enqueue 访问序列 C\nstep C 的邻居都访问过了，回退\n  pop S\n  mark G[C] done\nstep A 的邻居都访问过了，回退\n  pop S\n  mark G[A] done\nstep 遍历结束：A B D E C
title 深度优先搜索（从 A 出发）
steps 12 scenes 1
tl 0 0.000
st 0 0 cap=\ncells:S=\ncells:访问序列=\ngraph:G=A:normal:;B:normal:;C:normal:;D:normal:;E:normal:/A>B::normal;A>C::normal;B>D::normal;C>D::normal;D>E::normal\ncursor=0.000
st 0 1 cap=访问 A\ncells:S=A:new\ncells:访问序列=A:new\ngraph:G=A:active:;B:normal:;C:normal:;D:normal:;E:normal:/A>B::normal;A>C::normal;B>D::normal;C>D::normal;D>E::normal\ncursor=0.000

== dijkstra-zh
src kit dijkstra G="A-B:4, A-C:2, C-B:1, B-D:5, C-D:8, E" from=A lang=zh
exp title Dijkstra 最短路径（从 A 出发）\ngraph G = A-B:4, A-C:2, C-B:1, B-D:5, C-D:8, E\nstep 起点 A 的距离为 0，其余为 ∞\n  set G[A] = "0"\n  set G[B] = "∞"\n  set G[C] = "∞"\n  set G[D] = "∞"\n  set G[E] = "∞"\n  mark G[A] active\nstep 未确定的点里 A 最近（0），确定它\n  mark G[A] done\nstep 经 A 更新：B：∞ → 4、C：∞ → 2\n  mark G[A-B] active\n  set G[B] = "4"\n  mark G[B] active\n  mark G[A-C] active\n  set G[C] = "2"\n  mark G[C] active\nstep 未确定的点里 C 最近（2），确定它\n  mark G[C] done\n  mark G[A-C] done\nstep 经 C 更新：B：4 → 3、D：∞ → 10\n  unmark G[A-B]\n  mark G[C-B] active\n  set G[B] = "3"\n  mark G[B] active\n  mark G[C-D] active\n  set G[D] = "10"\n  mark G[D] active\nstep 未确定的点里 B 最近（3），确定它\n  mark G[B] done\n  mark G[C-B] done\nstep 经 B 更新：D：10 → 8\n  unmark G[C-D]\n  mark G[B-D] active\n  set G[D] = "8"\n  mark G[D] active\nstep 未确定的点里 D 最近（8），确定它\n  mark G[D] done\n  mark G[B-D] done\nstep 全部确定。E 不可达
title Dijkstra 最短路径（从 A 出发）
steps 10 scenes 1
tl 0 0.000
st 0 0 cap=\ngraph:G=A:normal:;B:normal:;C:normal:;D:normal:;E:normal:/A-B:4:normal;A-C:2:normal;C-B:1:normal;B-D:5:normal;C-D:8:normal\ncursor=0.000
st 0 1 cap=起点 A 的距离为 0，其余为 ∞\ngraph:G=A:active:0;B:normal:∞;C:normal:∞;D:normal:∞;E:normal:∞/A-B:4:normal;A-C:2:normal;C-B:1:normal;B-D:5:normal;C-D:8:normal\ncursor=0.000

== disk-sstf-zh
src kit disk alg=sstf start=100 requests=55,58,39,18,90,160,150,38,184 lang=zh
exp title 磁盘调度：SSTF（从 100 开始）\narray 磁道 = [18, 38, 39, 55, 58, 90, 100, 150, 160, 184]\npointer 磁头 -> 磁道[6]\nvar 移动距离 = 0\nqueue 访问序列 = []\nstep SSTF 最短寻找时间优先：每次去最近的请求；磁头在 100\n  mark 磁道[6] muted\nstep 离 100 最近的是 90；100 → 90，移动 10，累计 10\n  move 磁头 -> 磁道[5]\n  set 移动距离 = 10\n  mark 磁道[5] done\n  enqueue 访问序列 90\nstep 离 90 最近的是 58；90 → 58，移动 32，累计 42\n  move 磁头 -> 磁道[4]\n  set 移动距离 = 42\n  mark 磁道[4] done\n  enqueue 访问序列 58\nstep 离 58 最近的是 55；58 → 55，移动 3，累计 45\n  move 磁头 -> 磁道[3]\n  set 移动距离 = 45\n  mark 磁道[3] done\n  enqueue 访问序列 55\nstep 离 55 最近的是 39；55 → 39，移动 16，累计 61\n  move 磁头 -> 磁道[2]\n  set 移动距离 = 61\n  mark 磁道[2] done\n  enqueue 访问序列 39\nstep 离 39 最近的是 38；39 → 38，移动 1，累计 62\n  move 磁头 -> 磁道[1]\n  set 移动距离 = 62\n  mark 磁道[1] done\n  enqueue 访问序列 38\nstep 离 38 最近的是 18；38 → 18，移动 20，累计 82\n  move 磁头 -> 磁道[0]\n  set 移动距离 = 82\n  mark 磁道[0] done\n  enqueue 访问序列 18\nstep 离 18 最近的是 150；18 → 150，移动 132，累计 214\n  move 磁头 -> 磁道[7]\n  set 移动距离 = 214\n  mark 磁道[7] done\n  enqueue 访问序列 150\nstep 离 150 最近的是 160；150 → 160，移动 10，累计 224\n  move 磁头 -> 磁道[8]\n  set 移动距离 = 224\n  mark 磁道[8] done\n  enqueue 访问序列 160\nstep 离 160 最近的是 184；160 → 184，移动 24，累计 248\n  move 磁头 -> 磁道[9]\n  set 移动距离 = 248\n  mark 磁道[9] done\n  enqueue 访问序列 184\nstep 总移动 248 个磁道，平均寻道长度 248/9 = 27.56

== disk-scan-zh
src kit disk alg=scan start=100 requests=55,58,39,18,90,160,150,38,184 dir=down lang=zh
exp title 磁盘调度：SCAN（从 100 开始）\narray 磁道 = [0, 18, 38, 39, 55, 58, 90, 100, 150, 160, 184]\npointer 磁头 -> 磁道[7]\nvar 移动距离 = 0\nqueue 访问序列 = []\nstep SCAN 电梯：沿磁道号减小方向走到尽头再掉头；磁头在 100\n  mark 磁道[7] muted\nstep 沿磁道号减小方向继续；100 → 90，移动 10，累计 10\n  move 磁头 -> 磁道[6]\n  set 移动距离 = 10\n  mark 磁道[6] done\n  enqueue 访问序列 90\nstep 沿磁道号减小方向继续；90 → 58，移动 32，累计 42\n  move 磁头 -> 磁道[5]\n  set 移动距离 = 42\n  mark 磁道[5] done\n  enqueue 访问序列 58\nstep 沿磁道号减小方向继续；58 → 55，移动 3，累计 45\n  move 磁头 -> 磁道[4]\n  set 移动距离 = 45\n  mark 磁道[4] done\n  enqueue 访问序列 55\nstep 沿磁道号减小方向继续；55 → 39，移动 16，累计 61\n  move 磁头 -> 磁道[3]\n  set 移动距离 = 61\n  mark 磁道[3] done\n  enqueue 访问序列 39\nstep 沿磁道号减小方向继续；39 → 38，移动 1，累计 62\n  move 磁头 -> 磁道[2]\n  set 移动距离 = 62\n  mark 磁道[2] done\n  enqueue 访问序列 38\nstep 沿磁道号减小方向继续；38 → 18，移动 20，累计 82\n  move 磁头 -> 磁道[1]\n  set 移动距离 = 82\n  mark 磁道[1] done\n  enqueue 访问序列 18\nstep SCAN 要走到这一头的尽头 0 才掉头；18 → 0，移动 18，累计 100\n  move 磁头 -> 磁道[0]\n  set 移动距离 = 100\n  mark 磁道[0] active\nstep 掉头，沿反方向依次访问；0 → 150，移动 150，累计 250\n  unmark 磁道[0]\n  move 磁头 -> 磁道[8]\n  set 移动距离 = 250\n  mark 磁道[8] done\n  enqueue 访问序列 150\nstep 掉头，沿反方向依次访问；150 → 160，移动 10，累计 260\n  move 磁头 -> 磁道[9]\n  set 移动距离 = 260\n  mark 磁道[9] done\n  enqueue 访问序列 160\nstep 掉头，沿反方向依次访问；160 → 184，移动 24，累计 284\n  move 磁头 -> 磁道[10]\n  set 移动距离 = 284\n  mark 磁道[10] done\n  enqueue 访问序列 184\nstep 总移动 284 个磁道，平均寻道长度 284/9 = 31.56

== disk-cscan-zh
src kit disk alg=cscan start=100 requests=55,58,39,18,90,160,150,38,184 lang=zh
exp title 磁盘调度：C-SCAN（从 100 开始）\narray 磁道 = [0, 18, 38, 39, 55, 58, 90, 100, 150, 160, 184, 199]\npointer 磁头 -> 磁道[7]\nvar 移动距离 = 0\nqueue 访问序列 = []\nstep C-SCAN 循环扫描：只沿磁道号增大方向服务，到尽头后返回另一头；磁头在 100\n  mark 磁道[7] muted\nstep 沿磁道号增大方向继续；100 → 150，移动 50，累计 50\n  move 磁头 -> 磁道[8]\n  set 移动距离 = 50\n  mark 磁道[8] done\n  enqueue 访问序列 150\nstep 沿磁道号增大方向继续；150 → 160，移动 10，累计 60\n  move 磁头 -> 磁道[9]\n  set 移动距离 = 60\n  mark 磁道[9] done\n  enqueue 访问序列 160\nstep 沿磁道号增大方向继续；160 → 184，移动 24，累计 84\n  move 磁头 -> 磁道[10]\n  set 移动距离 = 84\n  mark 磁道[10] done\n  enqueue 访问序列 184\nstep C-SCAN 先走到尽头 199；184 → 199，移动 15，累计 99\n  move 磁头 -> 磁道[11]\n  set 移动距离 = 99\n  mark 磁道[11] active\nstep 立刻返回另一头 0，返回途中不服务（这段移动也计入）；199 → 0，移动 199，累计 298\n  unmark 磁道[11]\n  move 磁头 -> 磁道[0]\n  set 移动距离 = 298\n  mark 磁道[0] active\nstep 从头再沿磁道号增大方向访问；0 → 18，移动 18，累计 316\n  unmark 磁道[0]\n  move 磁头 -> 磁道[1]\n  set 移动距离 = 316\n  mark 磁道[1] done\n  enqueue 访问序列 18\nstep 从头再沿磁道号增大方向访问；18 → 38，移动 20，累计 336\n  move 磁头 -> 磁道[2]\n  set 移动距离 = 336\n  mark 磁道[2] done\n  enqueue 访问序列 38\nstep 从头再沿磁道号增大方向访问；38 → 39，移动 1，累计 337\n  move 磁头 -> 磁道[3]\n  set 移动距离 = 337\n  mark 磁道[3] done\n  enqueue 访问序列 39\nstep 从头再沿磁道号增大方向访问；39 → 55，移动 16，累计 353\n  move 磁头 -> 磁道[4]\n  set 移动距离 = 353\n  mark 磁道[4] done\n  enqueue 访问序列 55\nstep 从头再沿磁道号增大方向访问；55 → 58，移动 3，累计 356\n  move 磁头 -> 磁道[5]\n  set 移动距离 = 356\n  mark 磁道[5] done\n  enqueue 访问序列 58\nstep 从头再沿磁道号增大方向访问；58 → 90，移动 32，累计 388\n  move 磁头 -> 磁道[6]\n  set 移动距离 = 388\n  mark 磁道[6] done\n  enqueue 访问序列 90\nstep 总移动 388 个磁道，平均寻道长度 388/9 = 43.11

== cache-direct-zh
src kit cache map=direct lines=4 blocks=0,1,4,0,5,1,4 lang=zh
exp title Cache：直接映射，4 行\ngrid C rows=[行0, 行1, 行2, 行3] cols=[标记, 块] = 4x2\nvar 命中 = 0\nvar 缺失 = 0\nstep 直接映射：块 b 只能放第 b mod 4 行，标记 = b / 4；第 1 次访问块 0：0 mod 4 = 0 → 行0，标记 0；没有相同标记，缺失；行0 空着，调入\n  set C[0,0] = 0\n  set C[0,1] = 0\n  mark C[0,0..1] active\n  set 缺失 = 1\nstep 第 2 次访问块 1：1 mod 4 = 1 → 行1，标记 0；没有相同标记，缺失；行1 空着，调入\n  unmark C[0,0..1]\n  set C[1,0] = 0\n  set C[1,1] = 1\n  mark C[1,0..1] active\n  set 缺失 = 2\nstep 第 3 次访问块 4：4 mod 4 = 0 → 行0，标记 1；没有相同标记，缺失；换出最久没用的块 0（行0）\n  unmark C[1,0..1]\n  set C[0,0] = 1\n  set C[0,1] = 4\n  mark C[0,0..1] active\n  set 缺失 = 3\nstep 第 4 次访问块 0：0 mod 4 = 0 → 行0，标记 0；没有相同标记，缺失；换出最久没用的块 4（行0）\n  unmark C[0,0..1]\n  set C[0,0] = 0\n  set C[0,1] = 0\n  mark C[0,0..1] active\n  set 缺失 = 4\nstep 第 5 次访问块 5：5 mod 4 = 1 → 行1，标记 1；没有相同标记，缺失；换出最久没用的块 1（行1）\n  unmark C[0,0..1]\n  set C[1,0] = 1\n  set C[1,1] = 5\n  mark C[1,0..1] active\n  set 缺失 = 5\nstep 第 6 次访问块 1：1 mod 4 = 1 → 行1，标记 0；没有相同标记，缺失；换出最久没用的块 5（行1）\n  unmark C[1,0..1]\n  set C[1,0] = 0\n  set C[1,1] = 1\n  mark C[1,0..1] active\n  set 缺失 = 6\nstep 第 7 次访问块 4：4 mod 4 = 0 → 行0，标记 1；没有相同标记，缺失；换出最久没用的块 0（行0）\n  unmark C[1,0..1]\n  set C[0,0] = 1\n  set C[0,1] = 4\n  mark C[0,0..1] active\n  set 缺失 = 7\nstep 命中 0 次、缺失 7 次，命中率 0/7 = 0%\n  unmark C[0,0..1]
title Cache：直接映射，4 行

== cache-set-zh
src kit cache map=set lines=4 ways=2 blocks=0,1,4,0,5,1,8,4 replace=lru lang=zh
exp title Cache：2 路组相联，4 行，LRU 替换\ngrid C rows=[0组0路, 0组1路, 1组0路, 1组1路] cols=[标记, 块] = 4x2\nvar 命中 = 0\nvar 缺失 = 0\nstep 2 路组相联（2 组）：块 b 放第 b mod 2 组的任意一路，标记 = b / 2；第 1 次访问块 0：0 mod 2 = 0 → 第 0 组，标记 0；没有相同标记，缺失；0组0路 空着，调入\n  set C[0,0] = 0\n  set C[0,1] = 0\n  mark C[0,0..1] active\n  set 缺失 = 1\nstep 第 2 次访问块 1：1 mod 2 = 1 → 第 1 组，标记 0；没有相同标记，缺失；1组0路 空着，调入\n  unmark C[0,0..1]\n  set C[2,0] = 0\n  set C[2,1] = 1\n  mark C[2,0..1] active\n  set 缺失 = 2\nstep 第 3 次访问块 4：4 mod 2 = 0 → 第 0 组，标记 2；没有相同标记，缺失；0组1路 空着，调入\n  unmark C[2,0..1]\n  set C[1,0] = 2\n  set C[1,1] = 4\n  mark C[1,0..1] active\n  set 缺失 = 3\nstep 第 4 次访问块 0：0 mod 2 = 0 → 第 0 组，标记 0；0组0路 的标记相同，命中\n  unmark C[1,0..1]\n  mark C[0,0..1] done\n  set 命中 = 1\nstep 第 5 次访问块 5：5 mod 2 = 1 → 第 1 组，标记 2；没有相同标记，缺失；1组1路 空着，调入\n  unmark C[0,0..1]\n  set C[3,0] = 2\n  set C[3,1] = 5\n  mark C[3,0..1] active\n  set 缺失 = 4\nstep 第 6 次访问块 1：1 mod 2 = 1 → 第 1 组，标记 0；1组0路 的标记相同，命中\n  unmark C[3,0..1]\n  mark C[2,0..1] done\n  set 命中 = 2\nstep 第 7 次访问块 8：8 mod 2 = 0 → 第 0 组，标记 4；没有相同标记，缺失；换出最久没用的块 4（0组1路）\n  unmark C[2,0..1]\n  set C[1,0] = 4\n  set C[1,1] = 8\n  mark C[1,0..1] active\n  set 缺失 = 5\nstep 第 8 次访问块 4：4 mod 2 = 0 → 第 0 组，标记 2；没有相同标记，缺失；换出最久没用的块 0（0组0路）\n  unmark C[1,0..1]\n  set C[0,0] = 2\n  set C[0,1] = 4\n  mark C[0,0..1] active\n  set 缺失 = 6\nstep 命中 2 次、缺失 6 次，命中率 2/8 = 25%\n  unmark C[0,0..1]
title Cache：2 路组相联，4 行，LRU 替换
steps 10 scenes 1
tl 0 0.000
st 0 0 cap=\nvar:命中=0:normal\nvar:缺失=0:normal\ngrid:C=:normal,:normal;:normal,:normal;:normal,:normal;:normal,:normal\ncursor=0.000

== cache-full-zh
src kit cache map=full lines=3 blocks=1,2,3,1,4,2,5 replace=fifo lang=zh
exp title Cache：全相联，3 行，FIFO 替换\ngrid C rows=[行0, 行1, 行2] cols=[标记, 块] = 3x2\nvar 命中 = 0\nvar 缺失 = 0\nstep 全相联：块可以放任意一行，标记就是块号；第 1 次访问块 1：标记 1，查所有行；没有相同标记，缺失；行0 空着，调入\n  set C[0,0] = 1\n  set C[0,1] = 1\n  mark C[0,0..1] active\n  set 缺失 = 1\nstep 第 2 次访问块 2：标记 2，查所有行；没有相同标记，缺失；行1 空着，调入\n  unmark C[0,0..1]\n  set C[1,0] = 2\n  set C[1,1] = 2\n  mark C[1,0..1] active\n  set 缺失 = 2\nstep 第 3 次访问块 3：标记 3，查所有行；没有相同标记，缺失；行2 空着，调入\n  unmark C[1,0..1]\n  set C[2,0] = 3\n  set C[2,1] = 3\n  mark C[2,0..1] active\n  set 缺失 = 3\nstep 第 4 次访问块 1：标记 1，查所有行；行0 的标记相同，命中\n  unmark C[2,0..1]\n  mark C[0,0..1] done\n  set 命中 = 1\nstep 第 5 次访问块 4：标记 4，查所有行；没有相同标记，缺失；换出最早调入的块 1（行0）\n  unmark C[0,0..1]\n  set C[0,0] = 4\n  set C[0,1] = 4\n  mark C[0,0..1] active\n  set 缺失 = 4\nstep 第 6 次访问块 2：标记 2，查所有行；行1 的标记相同，命中\n  unmark C[0,0..1]\n  mark C[1,0..1] done\n  set 命中 = 2\nstep 第 7 次访问块 5：标记 5，查所有行；没有相同标记，缺失；换出最早调入的块 2（行1）\n  unmark C[1,0..1]\n  set C[1,0] = 5\n  set C[1,1] = 5\n  mark C[1,0..1] active\n  set 缺失 = 5\nstep 命中 2 次、缺失 5 次，命中率 2/7 = 28.57%\n  unmark C[1,0..1]

== gbn-ack-en
src kit gbn frames=6 window=4 lose=2,3 loseack=4 timeout=3 mod=8 lang=en
exp title Go-back-N (window 4)\nlanes Sender Receiver\nseq SW 0..5 window=4 at=Sender mod=8\nvar expected = 0\nstep send 0~3\n  send Sender -> Receiver "0"\n  timer Sender "T0" start\n  send Sender -> Receiver "1"\n  send Sender -> Receiver "2" lost\n  send Sender -> Receiver "3" lost\nstep Receiver: deliver 0, 1; reply ACK 0, ACK 1\n  deliver Receiver "0"\n  deliver Receiver "1"\n  set expected = 2\n  send Receiver -> Sender "ACK 0"\n  send Receiver -> Sender "ACK 1"\nstep got ACK 0, ACK 1; window slides to 2~5; send 4, 5\n  mark SW[0..1] done\n  timer Sender "T0" stop\n  timer Sender "T2" start\n  slide SW to=2\n  send Sender -> Receiver "4"\n  send Sender -> Receiver "5"\nstep Receiver: 4, 5 out of order, discarded; reply ACK 1, ACK 1 (duplicate ACKs)\n  discard Receiver "4"\n  discard Receiver "5"\n  send Receiver -> Sender "ACK 1"\n  send Receiver -> Sender "ACK 1"\nstep frame 2 times out: resend everything from 2\n  timer Sender "T2" expire\n  mark SW[2..5] active\n  send Sender -> Receiver "2"\n  timer Sender "T2" start\n  send Sender -> Receiver "3"\n  send Sender -> Receiver "4"\n  send Sender -> Receiver "5"\nstep Receiver: deliver 2~5; reply ACK 2, ACK 3, ACK 4, ACK 5\n  deliver Receiver "2"\n  deliver Receiver "3"\n  deliver Receiver "4"\n  deliver Receiver "5"\n  set expected = 6\n  send Receiver -> Sender "ACK 2"\n  send Receiver -> Sender "ACK 3"\n  send Receiver -> Sender "ACK 4" lost\n  send Receiver -> Sender "ACK 5"\nstep got ACK 2, ACK 3, ACK 5; every frame is acknowledged\n  mark SW[2..5] done\n  timer Sender "T2" stop

exp title Selective repeat (window 4)\nlanes Sender Receiver\nseq SW 0..7 window=4 at=Sender\nseq RW 0..7 window=4 at=Receiver\nstep send 0~3\n  send Sender -> Receiver "0"\n  timer Sender "T0" start\n  send Sender -> Receiver "1"\n  timer Sender "T1" start\n  send Sender -> Receiver "2" lost\n  timer Sender "T2" start\n  send Sender -> Receiver "3"\n  timer Sender "T3" start\nstep Receiver: deliver 0, 1; buffer 3; reply ACK 0, ACK 1, ACK 3\n  deliver Receiver "0"\n  unmark RW[0..0]\n  slide RW to=1\n  deliver Receiver "1"\n  unmark RW[1..1]\n  slide RW to=2\n  buffer Receiver "3"\n  mark RW[3] active\n  send Receiver -> Sender "ACK 0"\n  send Receiver -> Sender "ACK 1"\n  send Receiver -> Sender "ACK 3" lost\nstep got ACK 0, ACK 1; window slides to 2~5; send 4, 5\n  timer Sender "T0" stop\n  timer Sender "T1" stop\n  mark SW[0] done\n  mark SW[1] done\n  slide SW to=2\n  send Sender -> Receiver "4"\n  timer Sender "T4" start\n  send Sender -> Receiver "5"\n  timer Sender "T5" start\nstep Receiver: buffer 4, 5; reply ACK 4, ACK 5\n  buffer Receiver "4"\n  mark RW[4] active\n  buffer Receiver "5"\n  mark RW[5] active\n  send Receiver -> Sender "ACK 4"\n  send Receiver -> Sender "ACK 5"\nstep got ACK 4, ACK 5; frame 2, 3 times out: resend only that\n  timer Sender "T4" stop\n  timer Sender "T5" stop\n  mark SW[4] done\n  mark SW[5] done\n  timer Sender "T2" expire\n  mark SW[2] active\n  timer Sender "T3" expire\n  mark SW[3] active\n  send Sender -> Receiver "2"\n  timer Sender "T2" start\n  send Sender -> Receiver "3"\n  timer Sender "T3" start\nstep Receiver: deliver 2~5; 3 is a duplicate; reply ACK 2, ACK 3\n  deliver Receiver "2 3 4 5"\n  unmark RW[2..5]\n  slide RW to=6\n  note Receiver "dup 3" state=muted\n  send Receiver -> Sender "ACK 2"\n  send Receiver -> Sender "ACK 3"\nstep got ACK 2, ACK 3; window slides to 6~7; send 6, 7\n  timer Sender "T2" stop\n  timer Sender "T3" stop\n  mark SW[2] done\n  mark SW[3] done\n  slide SW to=6\n  send Sender -> Receiver "6" lost\n  timer Sender "T6" start\n  send Sender -> Receiver "7"\n  timer Sender "T7" start\nstep Receiver: buffer 7; reply ACK 7\n  buffer Receiver "7"\n  mark RW[7] active\n  send Receiver -> Sender "ACK 7"\nstep got ACK 7\n  timer Sender "T7" stop\n  mark SW[7] done\nstep frame 6 times out: resend only that\n  timer Sender "T6" expire\n  mark SW[6] active\n  send Sender -> Receiver "6"\n  timer Sender "T6" start\nstep Receiver: deliver 6, 7; reply ACK 6\n  deliver Receiver "6 7"\n  unmark RW[6..7]\n  send Receiver -> Sender "ACK 6"\nstep got ACK 6; every frame is acknowledged\n  timer Sender "T6" stop\n  mark SW[6] done

exp title Stop-and-wait\nlanes Sender Receiver\nseq SW 0..2 window=1 at=Sender\nvar expected = 0\nstep send 0\n  send Sender -> Receiver "0"\n  timer Sender "T0" start\nstep Receiver: deliver 0; reply ACK 0\n  deliver Receiver "0"\n  set expected = 1\n  send Receiver -> Sender "ACK 0"\nstep got ACK 0; window slides to 1; send 1\n  mark SW[0..0] done\n  timer Sender "T0" stop\n  slide SW to=1\n  send Sender -> Receiver "1" lost\n  timer Sender "T1" start\nstep frame 1 times out: resend only that\n  timer Sender "T1" expire\n  mark SW[1] active\n  send Sender -> Receiver "1"\n  timer Sender "T1" start\nstep Receiver: deliver 1; reply ACK 1\n  deliver Receiver "1"\n  set expected = 2\n  send Receiver -> Sender "ACK 1"\nstep got ACK 1; window slides to 2; send 2\n  mark SW[1..1] done\n  timer Sender "T1" stop\n  slide SW to=2\n  send Sender -> Receiver "2"\n  timer Sender "T2" start\nstep Receiver: deliver 2; reply ACK 2\n  deliver Receiver "2"\n  set expected = 3\n  send Receiver -> Sender "ACK 2" lost\nstep frame 2 times out: resend only that\n  timer Sender "T2" expire\n  mark SW[2] active\n  send Sender -> Receiver "2"\n  timer Sender "T2" start\nstep Receiver: 2 is a duplicate; reply ACK 2\n  discard Receiver "2"\n  send Receiver -> Sender "ACK 2"\nstep got ACK 2; every frame is acknowledged\n  mark SW[2..2] done\n  timer Sender "T2" stop

== bubble-en
src kit bubble A=[5, 1, 4, 2, 3] lang=en
exp title Bubble sort\narray A = [5, 1, 4, 2, 3]\npointer j -> A[0]\nstep Pass 1: 5 > 1: swap\n  mark A[0..1] active\n  move j -> A[0]\n  swap A[0] A[1]\nstep Pass 1: 5 > 4: swap\n  unmark A[0]\n  mark A[1..2] active\n  move j -> A[1]\n  swap A[1] A[2]\nstep Pass 1: 5 > 2: swap\n  unmark A[1]\n  mark A[2..3] active\n  move j -> A[2]\n  swap A[2] A[3]\nstep Pass 1: 5 > 3: swap; 5 is in place\n  unmark A[2]\n  mark A[3..4] active\n  move j -> A[3]\n  swap A[3] A[4]\n  unmark A[3]\n  mark A[4] done\nstep Pass 2: 1 ≤ 4: keep\n  mark A[0..1] active\n  move j -> A[0]\nstep Pass 2: 4 > 2: swap\n  unmark A[0]\n  mark A[1..2] active\n  move j -> A[1]\n  swap A[1] A[2]\nstep Pass 2: 4 > 3: swap; 4 is in place\n  unmark A[1]\n  mark A[2..3] active\n  move j -> A[2]\n  swap A[2] A[3]\n  unmark A[2]\n  mark A[3] done\nstep Pass 3: 1 ≤ 2: keep\n  mark A[0..1] active\n  move j -> A[0]\nstep Pass 3: 2 ≤ 3: keep; 3 is in place\n  unmark A[0]\n  mark A[1..2] active\n  move j -> A[1]\n  unmark A[1]\n  mark A[2] done\nstep No swaps in this pass: sorted\n  mark A done\n  move j -> null

== insertion-en
src kit insertion A=[b, d, a, c] lang=en
exp title Insertion sort\narray A = [b, d, a, c]\npointer key\nstep take A[1] = d and look for its place\n  move key -> A[1]\n  mark A[1] active\nstep b ≤ d: d goes here; the first 2 are sorted\n  unmark A[1]\nstep take A[2] = a and look for its place\n  move key -> A[2]\n  mark A[2] active\nstep d > a: shift d right\n  swap A[1] A[2]\n  move key -> A[1]\nstep b > a: shift b right\n  swap A[0] A[1]\n  move key -> A[0]\nstep reached the front: a goes first; the first 3 are sorted\n  unmark A[0]\nstep take A[3] = c and look for its place\n  move key -> A[3]\n  mark A[3] active\nstep d > c: shift d right\n  swap A[2] A[3]\n  move key -> A[2]\nstep b ≤ c: c goes here; the first 4 are sorted\n  unmark A[2]\nstep Sorted\n  mark A done\n  move key -> null

== quick-en

== crc-en
src kit crc data=101001 gen=1101 lang=en
exp title CRC：101001 / 1101\nsheet work\nwrite work[1,0] "1101"\nwrite work[1,4] ")"\nwrite work[1,5] "101001000"\nrule work[1,5..13] above\nmark work[1,11..13] new\nlabel work[0] "← quotient Q"\nlabel work[1] "← dividend: M followed by 3 zeros"\ntext "Divisor 1101 on the left; from the leftmost bit, align it and subtract modulo 2 (XOR) each time"\nstep G = 1101 is the polynomial x³ + x² + 1; its highest power is 3, so r = 3: the check bits R are 3 bits (= 4 bits of G − 1)\n  mark work[1,0..3] active\n  say "From left to right the bits stand for the powers 3, 2, …, 0 of x; a 1 means that term is present"\nstep append r = 3 zeros to M = 101001 (that is M × 2³): the dividend is 101001000\n  unmark work[1,0..3]\n  mark work[1,11..13] active\n  say "The 3 new bits are room for the remainder: filling them with it makes the whole frame divisible by G"\nstep Step 1: take 4 bits 1010: leading 1, so G fits: quotient bit 1, subtract (XOR) 1101 → 0111; bring down the next dividend bit 0 to make 4 bits again\n  unmark work[1,11..13]\n  mark work[1,5..8] active\n  write work[0,8] "1"\n  write work[2,5] "1101"\n  rule work[2,5..8]\n  write work[3,6] "111"\n  say "bit by bit (same → 0, different → 1): 1⊕1=0  0⊕1=1  1⊕0=1  0⊕1=1. The leading bit always becomes 0, so drop it and keep the last 3"\n  arrow work[1..3,9]\n  write work[3,9] "0"\nstep Step 2: take 4 bits 1110: leading 1, so G fits: quotient bit 1, subtract (XOR) 1101 → 0011; bring down the next dividend bit 1 to make 4 bits again\n  unmark work[1,5..8]\n  mark work[3,6..9] active\n  write work[0,9] "1"\n  write work[4,6] "1101"\n  rule work[4,6..9]\n  write work[5,7] "011"\n  say "bit by bit (same → 0, different → 1): 1⊕1=0  1⊕1=0  1⊕0=1  0⊕1=1. The leading bit always becomes 0, so drop it and keep the last 3"\n  arrow work[1..5,10]\n  write work[5,10] "1"\nstep Step 3: take 4 bits 0111: leading 0, so G does not fit: quotient bit 0, XOR 0000 changes nothing; bring down the next dividend bit 0 to make 4 bits again\n  unmark work[3,6..9]\n  mark work[5,7..10] active\n  write work[0,10] "0"\n  write work[6,7] "0000"\n  rule work[6,7..10]\n  write work[7,8] "111"\n  say "bit by bit (same → 0, different → 1): 0⊕0=0  1⊕0=1  1⊕0=1  1⊕0=1. The leading bit always becomes 0, so drop it and keep the last 3"\n  arrow work[1..7,11]\n  write work[7,11] "0"\nstep Step 4: take 4 bits 1110: leading 1, so G fits: quotient bit 1, subtract (XOR) 1101 → 0011; bring down the next dividend bit 0 to make 4 bits again\n  unmark work[5,7..10]\n  mark work[7,8..11] active\n  write work[0,11] "1"\n  write work[8,8] "1101"\n  rule work[8,8..11]\n  write work[9,9] "011"\n  say "bit by bit (same → 0, different → 1): 1⊕1=0  1⊕1=0  1⊕0=1  0⊕1=1. The leading bit always becomes 0, so drop it and keep the last 3"\n  arrow work[1..9,12]\n  write work[9,12] "0"\nstep Step 5: take 4 bits 0110: leading 0, so G does not fit: quotient bit 0, XOR 0000 changes nothing; bring down the next dividend bit 0 to make 4 bits again\n  unmark work[7,8..11]\n  mark work[9,9..12] active\n  write work[0,12] "0"\n  write work[10,9] "0000"\n  rule work[10,9..12]\n  write work[11,10] "110"\n  say "bit by bit (same → 0, different → 1): 0⊕0=0  1⊕0=1  1⊕0=1  0⊕0=0. The leading bit always becomes 0, so drop it and keep the last 3"\n  arrow work[1..11,13]\n  write work[11,13] "0"\nstep Step 6: take 4 bits 1100: leading 1, so G fits: quotient bit 1, subtract (XOR) 1101 → 0001\n  unmark work[9,9..12]\n  mark work[11,10..13] active\n  write work[0,13] "1"\n  write work[12,10] "1101"\n  rule work[12,10..13]\n  write work[13,11] "001"\n  say "bit by bit (same → 0, different → 1): 1⊕1=0  1⊕1=0  0⊕0=0  0⊕1=1. The leading bit always becomes 0, so drop it and keep the last 3"\nstep every dividend bit is used: the last 3 bits are the remainder R = 001 (always keep 3 bits); append it to M and send 101001001\n  unmark work[11,10..13]\n  mark work[13,11..13] done\n  label work[13] "← remainder R, the FCS"\n  say "The receiver divides the whole frame by 1101; remainder 0 means no error detected"

== dijkstra-en

== cache-full-en

exp title Cache: fully associative, 3 lines, FIFO replacement\ngrid C rows=[L0, L1, L2] cols=[tag, block] = 3x2\nvar hits = 0\nvar misses = 0\nstep fully associative: any line; the tag is the block number; access ＃1, block 1: tag 1, check every line; no matching tag: miss; L0 is empty, load it\n  set C[0,0] = 1\n  set C[0,1] = 1\n  mark C[0,0..1] active\n  set misses = 1\nstep access ＃2, block 2: tag 2, check every line; no matching tag: miss; L1 is empty, load it\n  unmark C[0,0..1]\n  set C[1,0] = 2\n  set C[1,1] = 2\n  mark C[1,0..1] active\n  set misses = 2\nstep access ＃3, block 3: tag 3, check every line; no matching tag: miss; L2 is empty, load it\n  unmark C[1,0..1]\n  set C[2,0] = 3\n  set C[2,1] = 3\n  mark C[2,0..1] active\n  set misses = 3\nstep access ＃4, block 1: tag 1, check every line; L0 has the same tag: hit\n  unmark C[2,0..1]\n  mark C[0,0..1] done\n  set hits = 1\nstep access ＃5, block 4: tag 4, check every line; no matching tag: miss; evict block 1, loaded earliest (L0)\n  unmark C[0,0..1]\n  set C[0,0] = 4\n  set C[0,1] = 4\n  mark C[0,0..1] active\n  set misses = 4\nstep access ＃6, block 2: tag 2, check every line; L1 has the same tag: hit\n  unmark C[0,0..1]\n  mark C[1,0..1] done\n  set hits = 2\nstep access ＃7, block 5: tag 5, check every line; no matching tag: miss; evict block 2, loaded earliest (L1)\n  unmark C[1,0..1]\n  set C[1,0] = 5\n  set C[1,1] = 5\n  mark C[1,0..1] active\n  set misses = 5\nstep 2 hits, 5 misses: hit rate 2/7 = 28.57%\n  unmark C[1,0..1]
